}

nhe47_md_sync_then_swap_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { nhe47_md_core_base_pair_valid = yes }
        nhe47_md_core_snapshot_effect = yes
        if = {
            limit = { nhe47_md_core_upgrade_requirements = yes }
            scope:actor = { nhe47_md_core_diag_begin_effect = yes }
            scope:actor = { set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_sync_busy value = yes } }
            nhe47_md_core_upgrade_effect = yes
            scope:actor = { nhe47_md_core_diag_record_effect = { PHASE = after_upgrade_before_swap } }
            if = {
                limit = { nhe47_md_core_assets_unchanged = yes nhe47_md_pair_same_main = yes scope:actor = { NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_sync_failed } } }
                # Main upgrades are permanent. The same-main kernel now exchanges attachments only.
                scope:actor = { remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_sync_busy remove_variable = nhe47_md_swap_completed }
                nhe47_md_dispatch_same_main_effect = yes
                scope:actor = { nhe47_md_core_diag_record_effect = { PHASE = after_swap } }
                scope:actor = {
                    if = { limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_swap_completed NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_swap_incomplete } } if = { limit = { debug_only = yes nhe47_md_validation_allowed = yes } nhe47_md_core_notice_effect = { TYPE = nhe47_md_core_done STATUS = nhe47_md_core_done_desc } } remove_variable = nhe47_md_swap_completed nhe47_md_core_clear_snapshot_effect = yes }
                    else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_swap_incomplete } } nhe47_md_core_notice_effect = { TYPE = nhe47_md_core_attachments_refused STATUS = nhe47_md_core_attachments_refused_desc } nhe47_md_core_clear_snapshot_effect = yes }
                    else = { nhe47_md_core_diag_fail_effect = yes nhe47_md_core_notice_effect = { TYPE = nhe47_md_core_failed STATUS = nhe47_md_core_failed_desc } }
                }
            }
            else = { nhe47_md_core_fail_effect = yes scope:actor = { nhe47_md_core_diag_fail_effect = yes nhe47_md_core_notice_effect = { TYPE = nhe47_md_core_failed STATUS = nhe47_md_core_failed_desc } } }
        }
        else = { scope:actor = { nhe47_md_core_notice_effect = { TYPE = nhe47_md_core_refused STATUS = nhe47_md_core_refused_desc } } }
    }
    else = { scope:actor = { trigger_event = nhe47_md.0202 } }
}

nhe47_md_core_read_observed_ranks_effect = {
    remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank
    remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank
    if = { limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_a }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_01 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_02 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_03 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 3 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_04 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 4 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_05 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 5 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_01 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_02 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_03 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 3 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_04 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 4 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_05 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 5 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_01 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_02 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_03 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 3 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_04 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 4 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_05 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 5 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_a = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_06 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank value = 6 } }
    }
    if = { limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_b }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_01 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_02 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_03 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 3 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_04 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 4 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = estate_main_05 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 5 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_01 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_02 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_03 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 3 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_04 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 4 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = east_asian_estate_main_05 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 5 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_01 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_02 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_03 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 3 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_04 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 4 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_05 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 5 } }
        if = { limit = { scope:nhe47_md_b = { has_domicile_building = japanese_manor_main_06 } } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank value = 6 } }
    }
    nhe47_md_extra_core_read_observed_ranks_effect = yes
}

nhe47_md_core_notice_effect = {
    nhe47_md_core_read_observed_ranks_effect = yes
    send_interface_message = {
        type = $TYPE$
        left_icon = this
        if = {
            limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_core_a_title exists = scope:nhe47_md_core_b_title }
            scope:nhe47_md_core_a_title = { save_scope_as = nhe47_md_message_core_a_title }
            scope:nhe47_md_core_b_title = { save_scope_as = nhe47_md_message_core_b_title }
            custom_tooltip = nhe47_md_core_pair_desc
        }
        else = { custom_tooltip = nhe47_md_core_titles_unknown_desc }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_a_rank has_variable = nhe47_md_core_b_rank }
            save_scope_value_as = { name = nhe47_md_message_core_a_before value = var:nhe47_md_core_a_rank }
            save_scope_value_as = { name = nhe47_md_message_core_b_before value = var:nhe47_md_core_b_rank }
            custom_tooltip = nhe47_md_core_before_desc
        }
        else = { custom_tooltip = nhe47_md_core_before_unknown_desc }
        if = {
            limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank has_variable = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank }
            save_scope_value_as = { name = nhe47_md_message_core_a_after value = var:nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank }
            save_scope_value_as = { name = nhe47_md_message_core_b_after value = var:nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank }
            custom_tooltip = nhe47_md_core_after_desc
        }
        else = { custom_tooltip = nhe47_md_core_after_unknown_desc }
        custom_tooltip = $STATUS$
    }
}

nhe47_md_core_diag_begin_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { nhe47_md_validation_allowed = yes NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_frozen } }
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_a
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_b
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_a_title
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_b_title
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_a_before
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_b_before
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_before_upgrade_a_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_before_upgrade_b_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_after_upgrade_before_swap_a_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_after_upgrade_before_swap_b_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_after_swap_a_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_after_swap_b_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_failed_a_rank
        remove_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_failed_b_rank
        if = { limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_a } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_a value = scope:nhe47_md_a } }
        if = { limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_core_a_title } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_a_title value = scope:nhe47_md_core_a_title } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_a_rank } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_a_before value = var:nhe47_md_core_a_rank } }
        if = { limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_b } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_b value = scope:nhe47_md_b } }
        if = { limit = { exists = scope:nhe47_md_core_b_title } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_b_title value = scope:nhe47_md_core_b_title } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_b_rank } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_b_before value = var:nhe47_md_core_b_rank } }
        nhe47_md_core_diag_record_effect = { PHASE = before_upgrade }
    }
}

nhe47_md_core_diag_record_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { nhe47_md_validation_allowed = yes NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_frozen } }
        nhe47_md_core_read_observed_ranks_effect = yes
        set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_phase value = flag:$PHASE$ }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_$PHASE$_a_rank value = var:nhe47_md_core_observed_a_rank } }
        if = { limit = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank } set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_$PHASE$_b_rank value = var:nhe47_md_core_observed_b_rank } }
    }
}

nhe47_md_core_diag_fail_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { nhe47_md_validation_allowed = yes NOT = { has_variable = nhe47_md_core_diag_frozen } }
        nhe47_md_core_diag_record_effect = { PHASE = failed }
        set_variable = { name = nhe47_md_core_diag_frozen value = yes }
    }
}
